### MARKET BOARD

_740 markets listed · 657 executable books rendered · 83 no-real-market placeholders suppressed · 0 silently omitted._

_**This is the complete executable board**: every market listed for this game that has a real book, with none held back for being thinly traded. Rows are in ladder order -- family, period, team, player, stat, threshold, ticker -- so the rungs of one ladder read together and can be compared. Volume, open interest and width are shown as information; they do not decide what appears._

_83 book(s) are held off the table as `no_real_market`: a 0.00/0.99-style quote with no volume and no open interest, where the midpoint is a quoting artefact and not a price you could pay. Every one of them is in `packet.json` under this game's `markets`, flagged and with its reason. They are the only kind of row allowed to be absent from the board above._

### CORRELATION GROUPS

- `2026_04_TEN_BAL::SCORING_UP` (same, strong (qualitative)): game total over, either team total over, QB passing yards over, receiver yards/receptions over, anytime TD yes, total TDs over — These rise and fall together with game scoring and pace.
- `2026_04_TEN_BAL::BAL_SIDE` (same, strong (qualitative)): BAL moneyline, BAL spread, BAL team total over, BAL player props over — Team-side markets share the same game-script risk.
- `2026_04_TEN_BAL::GAME_SCRIPT_OPPOSED` (opposed, moderate (qualitative)): favourite rushing volume over, favourite passing volume over — A team that leads runs more and throws less; volume props on the same team can conflict.

### KEY QUESTIONS FOR THE HANDICAPPER

1. 3 skill players are Questionable (Chris Moore (WR), Zay Flowers (WR), Tyjae Spears (RB)). These resolve at the inactive release 90 minutes before kickoff — is any current price worth taking before that, or is the option value of waiting larger than the move you expect?
2. The forecast changed since the previous capture but is not flagged material. Does the market appear to have reacted to it anyway?
3. KXNFLGAME-26OCT04TENBAL-BAL moved +0.215 since first capture. Is that move explained by public injury news already in this packet, or is it information we do not have?
4. The model disagrees by -0.610 on KXNFLREC-26OCT04TENBAL-TENCTATE14-3 (Carnell Tate). Is that a mean disagreement the market has historically handled better, or a genuine shape difference? Note the model was shown redundant to the market on player props.
5. The model disagrees by -0.592 on KXNFLREC-26OCT04TENBAL-TENCTATE14-2 (Carnell Tate). Is that a mean disagreement the market has historically handled better, or a genuine shape difference? Note the model was shown redundant to the market on player props.
6. KXNFLREC-26OCT04TENBAL-TENCTATE14-2 sits at a market price of 0.91 — a tail rung. The tail of the model's distribution is the least validated part of it. Is this rung relying on shape the model is known to miscalibrate?
